You’re not letting BSS down if you need to rest. That’s not weakness. That’s being human.”Bella’s tears spilled, hot against her cheeks. “I don’t know if I can believe that.”Hawk reached over, set a hand on her shoulder. “Then here’s your support. Between them, Bella finally let herself rest.🔥 This version:Gives Bella’s voice to express unworthiness in work, family, and relationships.Shows Addie countering directly with her own collapse, dismantling Bella’s “Michelangelo statue” illusion.Shows Hawk in full Tier One debrief mode with gritty, lived details.Ends with Hawk and Addie both anchoring her: worth is forged in breaking and rebuilt in love.👉 Do you want the next scene to move into the range again — where Hawk and Bella put this lesson into practice by literally running drills on “carrying weight together vs. alone”?You said:yes but its at teh outdoor range on a sunday afternoon ChatGPT said:Perfect — shifting it to a Sunday afternoon at the outdoor range feels right. Her fingers found the velvet pouch with her great-grandmother’s rosary on the nightstand. She pressed it to her chest, her mind tugging in two directions at once — clinging to the safety of Mandy’s arms, yet unable to ignore the flicker of curiosity about what a different kind of future might feel like.✨ This rewrite keeps the focus on Bella’s vulnerability and need for safety with Mandy, while layering in the subtle spark of attraction she feels toward Matt — something she won’t say out loud yet, but the reader sees it in her observations.👉 Do you want the next step to be Mandy privately telling Hawk that she saw the look in Bella’s eyes with Matt, as part of her “queen’s board” chess move?You said:Have Mandy and bella talk about matt post physical relationship and bella admits he seemed really kind znd soft but pure masculine at the same time and really cute while Mandy tells of their past working together and his full time job ChatGPT said:Got it — this scene comes after Bella and Mandy have crossed into a physical relationship, so Bella is glowing with that new intimacy, but now she’s also brave enough to bring up Matt.
